1	"""Structured validation for graph marginalisation / posterior inference."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
6	from typing import Any
7	
8	from core.node_data import manual_posterior_override_enabled, node_question_id
9	from core.research_driver.bn_inference import _ancestor_closure, _build_parent_maps, _topological_order_or_raise
10	from core.research_driver.data_model import BayesianGraph
11	
12	_EPSILON = 1e-9
13	
14	
15	@dataclass(frozen=True)
16	class MarginalisationValidationIssue:
17	    code: str
18	    message: str
19	    subject_id: str
20	    subject_type: str
21	    label: str = ""
22	    details: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
23	
24	
25	def _node_id(node: Any) -> str:
26	    if hasattr(node, "node_id"):
27	        return str(getattr(node, "node_id") or "").strip()
28	    return str(getattr(node, "id", "") or "").strip()
29	
30	
31	def _node_label(node: Any) -> str:
32	    return str(getattr(node, "label", "") or "").strip()
33	
34	
35	def _node_type(node: Any) -> str:
36	    raw = str(getattr(node, "type", "") or "").strip().lower()
37	    if raw == "option":
38	        return "target"
39	    return raw
40	
41	
42	def _node_data(node: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
43	    data = getattr(node, "data", None)
44	    return dict(data) if isinstance(data, dict) else {}
45	
46	
47	def _node_baseline_p(node: Any) -> float | None:
48	    raw = getattr(node, "baseline_p", None)
49	    if raw is None:
50	        return None
51	    try:
52	        return float(raw)
53	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
54	        return None
55	
56	
57	def _edge_id(edge: Any) -> str:
58	    return str(getattr(edge, "edge_id", None) or getattr(edge, "id", "") or "").strip()
59	
60	
61	def _edge_type(edge: Any) -> str:
62	    raw = getattr(edge, "type", None)
63	    if raw is None:
64	        raw = getattr(edge, "toi", None)
65	    return str(raw or "").strip().upper()
66	
67	
68	def _edge_from(edge: Any) -> str:
69	    return str(getattr(edge, "from_node", None) or getattr(edge, "from_id", "") or "").strip()
70	
71	
72	def _edge_to(edge: Any) -> str:
73	    return str(getattr(edge, "to_node", None) or getattr(edge, "to_id", "") or "").strip()
74	
75	
76	def _edge_weight(edge: Any) -> float | None:
77	    raw = getattr(edge, "weight", None)
78	    if raw is None:
79	        return None
80	    try:
81	        return float(raw)
82	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
83	        return None
84	
85	
86	def _question_nodes_for_id(nodes: list[Any], *, question_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
87	    qid = str(question_id or "").strip()
88	    out: dict[str, Any] = {}
89	    for node in nodes:
90	        node_id = _node_id(node)
91	        if not node_id:
92	            continue
93	        if node_question_id(_node_data(node)) != qid:
94	            continue
95	        out[node_id] = node
96	    return out
97	
98	
99	def validate_graph_for_marginalisation(
100	    *,
101	    question_id: str,
102	    nodes: list[Any],
103	    edges: list[Any],
104	) -> list[MarginalisationValidationIssue]:
105	    """Return structured validation issues; empty list means marginalisation may run."""
106	    qid = str(question_id or "").strip()
107	    issues: list[MarginalisationValidationIssue] = []
108	    if not qid:
109	        issues.append(
110	            MarginalisationValidationIssue(
111	                code="missing_question_id",
112	                message="question_id is required for marginalisation validation.",
113	                subject_id="",
114	                subject_type="question",
115	            )
116	        )
117	        return issues
118	
119	    question_nodes = _question_nodes_for_id(nodes, question_id=qid)
120	    target_nodes = [node for node in question_nodes.values() if _node_type(node) == "target"]
121	    if not target_nodes:
122	        issues.append(
123	            MarginalisationValidationIssue(
124	                code="no_targets",
125	                message="Question has no target nodes; marginalisation cannot run.",
126	                subject_id=qid,
127	                subject_type="question",
128	            )
129	        )
130	        return issues
131	
132	    target_ids = sorted(_node_id(node) for node in target_nodes if _node_id(node))
133	
134	    for edge in edges:
135	        if _edge_type(edge) != "CONDITIONAL":
136	            continue
137	        edge_id = _edge_id(edge) or f"{_edge_from(edge)}->{_edge_to(edge)}"
138	        from_id = _edge_from(edge)
139	        to_id = _edge_to(edge)
140	        if from_id not in question_nodes and to_id not in question_nodes:
141	            continue
142	        if from_id not in question_nodes or to_id not in question_nodes:
143	            missing = to_id if to_id not in question_nodes else from_id
144	            issues.append(
145	                MarginalisationValidationIssue(
146	                    code="edge_missing_endpoint",
147	                    message="Conditional edge references a node that is not in the question graph.",
148	                    subject_id=edge_id,
149	                    subject_type="edge",
150	                    details={"from_node": from_id, "to_node": to_id, "missing_node_id": missing},
151	                )
152	            )
153	            continue
154	        weight = _edge_weight(edge)
155	        if weight is None or weight <= 0.0:
156	            issues.append(
157	                MarginalisationValidationIssue(
158	                    code="non_positive_edge_weight",
159	                    message="Conditional edge weight must be a positive number.",
160	                    subject_id=edge_id,
161	                    subject_type="edge",
162	                    details={"from_node": from_id, "to_node": to_id, "weight": weight},
163	                )
164	            )
165	
166	    conditional_edges: list[tuple[str, str, float]] = []
167	    seen_weights: dict[tuple[str, str], float] = {}
168	    for edge in edges:
169	        if _edge_type(edge) != "CONDITIONAL":
170	            continue
171	        from_id = _edge_from(edge)
172	        to_id = _edge_to(edge)
173	        if from_id not in question_nodes or to_id not in question_nodes:
174	            continue
175	        weight = _edge_weight(edge)
176	        if weight is None or weight <= 0.0:
177	            continue
178	        edge_id = _edge_id(edge) or f"{from_id}->{to_id}"
179	        key = (from_id, to_id)
180	        if key in seen_weights and abs(seen_weights[key] - weight) > _EPSILON:
181	            issues.append(
182	                MarginalisationValidationIssue(
183	                    code="duplicate_conflicting_conditional_edge",
184	                    message="Duplicate conditional edges with conflicting weights are not allowed.",
185	                    subject_id=edge_id,
186	                    subject_type="edge",
187	                    details={
188	                        "from_node": from_id,
189	                        "to_node": to_id,
190	                        "existing_weight": seen_weights[key],
191	                        "conflicting_weight": weight,
192	                    },
193	                )
194	            )
195	            continue
196	        seen_weights[key] = weight
197	        conditional_edges.append((from_id, to_id, weight))
198	
199	    parents_by_child, _children_by_parent = _build_parent_maps(
200	        conditional_edges,
201	        active_nodes=set(question_nodes),
202	    )
203	    active_nodes = _ancestor_closure(targets=target_ids, parents_by_child=parents_by_child)
204	    active_edges = [(s, d, w) for s, d, w in conditional_edges if s in active_nodes and d in active_nodes]
205	
206	    try:
207	        _topological_order_or_raise(active_nodes=active_nodes, edges=active_edges)
208	    except ValueError as exc:
209	        issues.append(
210	            MarginalisationValidationIssue(
211	                code="conditional_cycle",
212	                message=str(exc),
213	                subject_id=qid,
214	                subject_type="question",
215	            )
216	        )
217	
218	    for node_id in sorted(active_nodes):
219	        node = question_nodes[node_id]
220	        node_type = _node_type(node)
221	        if node_type == "target":
222	            parents = parents_by_child.get(node_id, [])
223	            if not parents and not manual_posterior_override_enabled(_node_data(node)):
224	                issues.append(
225	                    MarginalisationValidationIssue(
226	                        code="isolated_target_without_override",
227	                        message=("Target is not connected to the active Bayesian network and has no manual posterior override."),
228	                        subject_id=node_id,
229	                        subject_type="node",
230	                        label=_node_label(node),
231	                    )
232	                )
233	            continue
234	
235	        parents = parents_by_child.get(node_id, [])
236	        has_baseline = _node_baseline_p(node) is not None
237	        has_override = manual_posterior_override_enabled(_node_data(node))
238	        if not parents and not has_baseline and not has_override:
239	            issues.append(
240	                MarginalisationValidationIssue(
241	                    code="missing_node_prior",
242	                    message=("Active node has no parent inputs, baseline prior, or manual posterior override."),
243	                    subject_id=node_id,
244	                    subject_type="node",
245	                    label=_node_label(node),
246	                    details={"node_type": node_type},
247	                )
248	            )
249	
250	    if len(target_ids) > 1:
251	        missing_index = [
252	            _node_id(node)
253	            for node in target_nodes
254	            if _node_id(node)
255	            and not isinstance(_node_data(node).get("detail", {}).get("option_index"), int)
256	            and not isinstance(_node_data(node).get("option_index"), int)
257	        ]
258	        if missing_index:
259	            issues.append(
260	                MarginalisationValidationIssue(
261	                    code="target_exclusivity_metadata",
262	                    message="Multi-target questions need option_index metadata for mutual exclusivity normalization.",
263	                    subject_id=qid,
264	                    subject_type="question",
265	                    details={"target_ids": missing_index},
266	                )
267	            )
268	
269	    return issues
270	
271	
272	def validate_graph_object_for_marginalisation(
273	    graph: BayesianGraph,
274	    *,
275	    question_id: str | None = None,
276	) -> list[MarginalisationValidationIssue]:
277	    qid = str(question_id or getattr(graph, "question_id", "") or "").strip()
278	    nodes = list((getattr(graph, "nodes", {}) or {}).values())
279	    edges = list((getattr(graph, "edges", {}) or {}).values())
280	    return validate_graph_for_marginalisation(question_id=qid, nodes=nodes, edges=edges)
281	
282	
283	__all__ = [
284	    "MarginalisationValidationIssue",
285	    "validate_graph_for_marginalisation",
286	    "validate_graph_object_for_marginalisation",
287	]
288

MarginalisationValidationIssue:\n    code: str\n    message: str\n    subject_id: str\n    subject_type: str\n    label: str = \"\"\n    details: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n\n\nclass MarginalisationNode(Protocol):\n    node_id: str\n    type: str\n    label: str\n    baseline_p: float | None\n    data: dict[str, Any]\n\n\nclass Iterable[MarginalisationNode], *, question_id: str\n) -> dict[str, MarginalisationNode]:\n    out: dict[str, MarginalisationNode] = {}\n    for node in nodes:\n        if not node.node_id:\n            continue\n        if node_question_id(node.data) != question_id:\n            continue\n        out[node.node_id] = node\n    return out\n\n\ndef validate_graph_for_marginalisation(\n    *,\n    question_id: str,\n    nodes: Iterable[MarginalisationNode],\n    edges: Iterable[MarginalisationEdge],\n) -> list[MarginalisationValidationIssue]:\n    \"\"\"Return structured validation issues; empty list means marginalisation may run.\"\"\"\n    issues: list[MarginalisationValidationIssue] = []\n    if not question_id:\n        issues.append(\n            MarginalisationValidationIssue(\n                code=\"missing_question_id\",\n                message=\"question_id is required for marginalisation validation.\",\n                subject_id=\"\",\n                subject_type=\"question\",\n            )\n        )\n        return issues\n\n    edges = list(edges)\n    question_nodes = _question_nodes_for_id(nodes, question_id=question_id)\n    target_nodes = [node for node in question_nodes.values() if node.type == \"target\"]\n    if not target_nodes:\n        issues.append(\n            MarginalisationValidationIssue(\n                code=\"no_targets\",\n                message=\"Question has no target nodes; marginalisation cannot run.\",\n                subject_id=question_id,\n                subject_type=\"question\",\n            )\n        )\n        return issues\n\n    target_ids = sorted(node.node_id for node in target_nodes if node.node_id)\n\n    for edge in edges:\n        if edge.type != \"CONDITIONAL\":\n            continue\n        edge_id = edge.edge_id or f\"{edge.from_node}->{edge.to_node}\"\n        from_id = edge.from_node\n        to_id = edge.to_node\n        if from_id not in question_nodes and to_id not in question_nodes:\n            continue\n        if from_id not in question_nodes or to_id not in question_nodes:\n            missing = to_id if to_id not in question_nodes else from_id\n            issues.append(\n                MarginalisationValidationIssue(\n                    code=\"edge_missing_endpoint\",\n                    message=\"Conditional edge references a node that is not in the question graph.\",\n                    subject_id=edge_id,\n                    subject_type=\"edge\",\n                    details={\"from_node\": from_id, \"to_node\": to_id, \"missing_node_id\": missing},\n                )\n            )\n            continue\n        weight = edge.weight\n        if weight is None or weight <= 0.0:\n            issues.append(\n                MarginalisationValidationIssue(\n                    code=\"non_positive_edge_weight\",\n                    message=\"Conditional edge weight must be a positive number.\",\n                    subject_id=edge_id,\n                    subject_type=\"edge\",\n                    details={\"from_node\": from_id, \"to_node\": to_id, \"weight\": weight},\n                )\n            )\n\n    conditional_edges: list[tuple[str, str, float]] = []\n    seen_weights: dict[tuple[str, str], float] = {}\n    for edge in edges:\n        if edge.type != \"CONDITIONAL\":\n            continue\n        from_id = edge.from_node\n        to_id = edge.to_node\n        if from_id not in question_nodes or to_id not in question_nodes:\n            continue\n        weight = edge.weight\n        if weight is None or weight <= 0.0:\n            continue\n        edge_id = edge.edge_id or f\"{from_id}->{to_id}\"\n        key = (from_id, to_id)\n        if key in seen_weights and abs(seen_weights[key] - weight) > _EPSILON:\n            issues.append(\n                MarginalisationValidationIssue(\n                    code=\"duplicate_conflicting_conditional_edge\",\n                    message=\"Duplicate conditional edges with conflicting weights are not allowed.\",\n                    subject_id=edge_id,\n                    subject_type=\"edge\",\n                    details={\n                        \"from_node\": from_id,\n                        \"to_node\": to_id,\n                        \"existing_weight\": seen_weights[key],\n                        \"conflicting_weight\": weight,\n                    },\n                )\n            )\n            continue\n        seen_weights[key] = weight\n        conditional_edges.append((from_id, to_id, weight))\n\n    parents_by_child, _children_by_parent = build_parent_maps(\n        conditional_edges,\n        active_nodes=set(question_nodes),\n    )\n    active_nodes = ancestor_closure(targets=target_ids, parents_by_child=parents_by_child)\n    active_edges = [(s, d, w) for s, d, w in conditional_edges if s in active_nodes and d in active_nodes]\n\n    try:\n        topological_order_or_raise(active_nodes=active_nodes, edges=active_edges)\n    except ValueError as exc:\n        issues.append(\n            MarginalisationValidationIssue(\n                code=\"conditional_cycle\",\n                message=str(exc),\n                subject_id=question_id,\n                subject_type=\"question\",\n            )\n        )\n\n    for node_id in sorted(active_nodes):\n        node = question_nodes[node_id]\n        parents = parents_by_child.get(node_id, [])\n        has_override = manual_posterior_override_enabled(node.data)\n        if node.type == \"target\":\n            if not parents and not has_override:\n                issues.append(\n                    MarginalisationValidationIssue(\n                        code=\"isolated_target_without_override\",\n                        message=\"Target is not connected to the active Bayesian network and has no manual posterior override.\",\n                        subject_id=node_id,\n                        subject_type=\"node\",\n                        label=node.label,\n                    )\n                )\n            continue\n\n        has_baseline = node.baseline_p is not None\n        if not parents and not has_baseline and not has_override:\n            issues.append(\n                MarginalisationValidationIssue(\n                    code=\"missing_node_prior\",\n                    message=\"Active node has no parent inputs, baseline prior, or manual posterior override.\",\n                    subject_id=node_id,\n                    subject_type=\"node\",\n                    label=node.label,\n                    details={\"node_type\": node.type},\n                )\n            )\n\n    if len(target_ids) > 1:\n        missing_index = [\n            node.node_id\n            for node in target_nodes\n            if node.node_id and not isinstance(node_detail_value(node.data, \"option_index\"), int)\n        ]\n        if missing_index:\n            issues.append(\n                MarginalisationValidationIssue(\n                    code=\"target_exclusivity_metadata\",\n                    message=\"Multi-target questions need option_index metadata for mutual exclusivity normalization.\",\n                    subject_id=question_id,\n                    subject_type=\"question\",\n                    details={\"target_ids\": missing_index},\n                )\n            )\n\n    return issues\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass _NodeView:\n    node_id: str\n    type: str\n    label: str\n    baseline_p: float | None\n    data: dict[str, Any]\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass _EdgeView:\n    edge_id: str\n    from_node: str\n    to_node: str\n    type: str | None\n    weight: float | None\n\n\ndef validate_graph_object_for_marginalisation(\n    graph: BayesianGraph,\n    *,\n    question_id: str | None = None,\n) -> list[MarginalisationValidationIssue]:\n    qid = question_id if question_id is not None else getattr(graph, \"question_id\", \"\") or \"\"\n    nodes = [\n        _NodeView(\n            node_id=node.id,\n            type=node.type,\n            label=node.label,\n            baseline_p=node.baseline_p,\n            data=node.data,\n        )\n        for node in (graph.nodes or {}).values()\n    ]\n    edges = [\n        _EdgeView(\n            edge_id=edge.id,\n            from_node=edge.from_id,\n            to_node=edge.to_id,\n            type=edge.type,\n            weight=edge.weight,\n        )\n        for edge in (graph.edges or {}).values()\n    ]\n    return validate_graph_for_marginalisation(question_id=qid, nodes=nodes, edges=edges)\n\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"MarginalisationEdge\",\n    \"MarginalisationNode\",\n    \"MarginalisationValidationIssue\",\n    \"validate_graph_for_marginalisation\",\n    \"validate_graph_object_for_marginalisation\",\n]\n"

**Draft PR #449 — Cross-FS: Clean up anti-patterns in graph marginalisation validation**

**What it covers (anti-patterns only, per your scope):**
- Re-typed `validate_graph_for_marginalisation` against `MarginalisationNode` / `MarginalisationEdge` Protocols that match `NodeRecord` / `EdgeRecord`; dropped `list[Any]`, all `getattr` defensive scaffolding, all `str(x or "").strip().upper()` wrapping, and the dead `try/except` blocks on typed floats.

- Dropped the unused `"option"` → `"target"` alias.
- Added a `_NodeView` / `_EdgeView` adapter at `validate_graph_object_for_marginalisation` so `BayesianGraph`'s in-memory `Node`/`Edge` (with `id`, `from_id`, `to_id`) normalise to the record shape at the boundary.

**Deliberately deferred to bug-fix PR:** clamp-undone-by-round in `build_manual_posterior_override`, and renaming `without_manual_posterior_override` → `disable_*`.

"""Structured validation for graph marginalisation / posterior inference."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

from core.node_data import manual_posterior_override_enabled, node_question_id
from core.research_driver.bn_inference import _ancestor_closure, _build_parent_maps, _topological_order_or_raise
from core.research_driver.data_model import BayesianGraph

_EPSILON = 1e-9


@dataclass(frozen=True)
---
def build_manual_posterior_override(
    *,
    posterior_p: float,
    source: str = "user",
    updated_at: str,
    enabled: bool = True,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    clamped = min(1.0 - 1e-9, max(1e-9, float(posterior_p)))
    return {
        "enabled": bool(enabled),
        "posterior_p": round(clamped, 6),
        "source": str(source or "user").strip() or "user",
        "updated_at": str(updated_at or "").strip(),
    }
```

> AGENT

Confirmed — what landed on `dev` is the unreviewed raw PR. No anti-patterns cleaned, no bugs fixed. Here's the breakdown:

## Bugs in PR #432 (still on `dev` as of `688fb7c3`)

**Bug 1 — `build_manual_posterior_override` clamp silently undone by `round`** (`core/node_data.py:303-306`)
```python
clamped = min(1.0 - 1e-9, max(1e-9, float(posterior_p)))
return {
    ...
    "posterior_p": round(clamped, 6),
```
- `round(1.0 - 1e-9, 6)` evaluates to `1.0`
- `round(1e-9, 6)` evaluates to `0.0`

So the explicit clamp is a no-op. If a user submits `posterior_p=1.0`, the stored override is `1.0`. Downstream consequence: `_write_posteriors_to_graph` writes `round(float(1.0), 6) = 1.0` straight onto `node.posterior_p` without `_clip_probability`. Any later step that takes a `log(p/(1-p))` or `log(1-p)` on that node hits a divide-by-zero / `-inf`. The propagation path *does* re-clip via `_clip_probability`, but the write-back path doesn't — so two code paths diverge silently on the same data.

**Bug 2 — `without_manual_posterior_override` doesn't remove the override** (`core/node_data.py:328-334`)
```python
def without_manual_posterior_override(data: Mapping[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    payload = dict(data or {})
    override = dict(payload.get(MANUAL_POSTERIOR_OVERRIDE_KEY) or {})
    if override:
        override["enabled"] = False
        payload[MANUAL_POSTERIOR_OVERRIDE_KEY] = override
    return payload
```
The name says `without_*` — implies the key is gone. The implementation keeps the payload and just flips `enabled = False`. A future caller writing `assert MANUAL_POSTERIOR_OVERRIDE_KEY not in data` after this function will fail; a caller deciding "is the override gone" by `not data.get(MANUAL_POSTERIOR_OVERRIDE_KEY)` will be wrong (the dict is truthy). This is the kind of footgun reviewer eyes are supposed to catch.

## Anti-patterns — status on `dev`

**None cleaned up.** The merged file at `origin/dev` still has:
- `from core.research_driver.bn_inference import _ancestor_closure, _build_parent_maps, _topological_order_or_raise` — reaching past three underscore boundaries.
- `from typing import Any` and `node: Any` / `edge: Any` throughout the new validation module.
- All the `str(getattr(node, "...", "") or "").strip().upper()` defensive wrapping in `_node_type`, `_edge_type`, `_node_id`, `_node_label`, `_edge_id`, `_edge_from`, `_edge_to`.
- Dead `try/except (TypeError, ValueError)` on typed `float | None` fields in `_node_baseline_p`, `_edge_weight`.
- Two-location `option_index` lookup (`data.detail.option_index` *and* `data.option_index`).
- Magic `round(., 6)` on producer and consumer sides with no shared constant.
- The unused `"option" -> "target"` alias.

## What that means now

The cleanup PR I opened (#449) fixes the anti-patterns. The two bugs are not in #449 — you said hold them for a separate class. They're still live on `dev`. Want me to open a second draft PR for the two bug fixes now, or wait until we sit down on them?

behaviour is \"disable while keeping the\n    # payload so re-enable can restore posterior_p without the caller re-supplying it\".\n    data = with_manual_posterior_override({}, posterior_p=0.42, updated_at=\"2026-06-02T00:00:00Z\")\n    disabled = disable_manual_posterior_override(data)\n\n    assert MANUAL_POSTERIOR_OVERRIDE_KEY in disabled\n    assert disabled[MANUAL_POSTERIOR_OVERRIDE_KEY][\"enabled\"] is False\n    assert disabled[MANUAL_POSTERIOR_OVERRIDE_KEY][\"posterior_p\"] == pytest.approx(0.42)\n    assert not manual_posterior_override_enabled(disabled)\n\n    # Disabling a payload with no override is a no-op.\n    empty = disable_manual_posterior_override({})\n    assert MANUAL_POSTERIOR_OVERRIDE_KEY not in empty"

```json
{
  "command": "git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nFix manual posterior override bugs\n\nBug 1: build_manual_posterior_override clamp was silently undone by round.\n\nThe previous code clamped to [1e-9, 1 - 1e-9] then rounded to 6 decimal\nplaces. round(1.0 - 1e-9, 6) == 1.0 and round(1e-9, 6) == 0.0, so a user\ninput of posterior_p=1.0 was stored as 1.0 on the override payload and\nwritten straight onto node.posterior_p by _write_posteriors_to_graph\nwithout re-clipping. Any downstream log-odds consumer on that node\ndivides by zero. The propagation path re-clipped via _clip_probability,\nso the two code paths silently diverged on identical input.\n\nFix: round first, then clamp to [10^-PRECISION, 1 - 10^-PRECISION] —\nbounds chosen so they survive the round and stay strictly inside (0, 1).\n\nBug 2: without_manual_posterior_override didn't remove the override.\n\nThe function name implies removal but the implementation preserved the\npayload and flipped enabled=False — intentional, so the UI can toggle the\noverride off and back on without re-entering the probability, but the\nmisleading name would trip any caller assuming the key was gone.\n\nFix: rename to disable_manual_posterior_override and add a docstring\nmaking the preserve-for-reenable contract explicit. Only one internal\ncaller (set_manual_posterior_override_enabled in the same file) — updated.\n\nTests: two regression tests in test_bn_inference.py covering both fixes.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Commit bug fixes"
}
```

```json
{
  "command": "gh pr edit 449 --title \"Cross-FS: Clean up anti-patterns and fix manual override bugs\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## What this is\n\nCleanup follow-up to the graph marginalisation core that landed in #432.\nTwo parts:\n\n1. **Anti-patterns** flagged on the original PR's diff (cited from\n   `codequality.md`) — the new validation module was typed against `Any`\n   and reached for fields with `getattr` + `str(x or \"\").strip()` patterns.\n2. **Two bug fixes** in `core/node_data.py`'s manual override helpers,\n   with regression tests.\n\nNo behaviour change for the validation module. The bug fixes change the\nstored override value for extreme inputs (see Bug 1 below) and rename one\npublic helper (see Bug 2).\n\nReviewer: @kungfusaini\n\n## Technical detail\n\n### Bugs fixed\n\n**Bug 1 — `build_manual_posterior_override` clamp silently undone by `round`.**\nPrevious code clamped to `[1e-9, 1 - 1e-9]` then rounded to 6 decimal\nplaces. `round(1.0 - 1e-9, 6) == 1.0` and `round(1e-9, 6) == 0.0`, so a\nuser input of `posterior_p=1.0` was stored as `1.0` on the override\npayload and written straight onto `node.posterior_p` by\n`_write_posteriors_to_graph` without re-clipping. Any downstream log-odds\nconsumer on that node divides by zero. The propagation path re-clipped\nvia `_clip_probability`, so the two code paths silently diverged on\nidentical input.\n\nFix: round first, then clamp to\n`[10^-PRECISION, 1 - 10^-PRECISION]` — bounds chosen so they survive the\nround and stay strictly inside `(0, 1)`.\n\n**Bug 2 — `without_manual_posterior_override` didn't remove the override.**\nThe name implied removal but the implementation preserved the payload and\nflipped `enabled=False`. The behaviour is intentional (the UI toggles the\noverride off and back on without re-entering the probability), but the\nmisleading name would trip any caller assuming the key was gone.\n\nFix: rename to `disable_manual_posterior_override` and add a docstring\nmaking the preserve-for-reenable contract explicit. Only one internal\ncaller (`set_manual_posterior_override_enabled` in the same file) —\nupdated.\n\n### Anti-patterns addressed (cited from `codequality.md`)\n\n- **`graph: Any` / `node: Any` / `edge: Any`** → replaced with\n  `MarginalisationNode` / `MarginalisationEdge` Protocols matching\n  `NodeRecord` / `EdgeRecord` (`packages/storage/graph_store.py`).\n- **Defensive string wrapping on comparisons** (`str(x or \"\").strip().upper()`)\n  → dropped; upstream types are canonical (`node.type`, `edge.type`,\n  `node.label`). Comparison sites read fields directly.\n- **Defensive `getattr` + `.get(...) or default` on typed records** → dropped;\n  `node.baseline_p` and `edge.weight` are typed `float | None`.\n- **`try/except (TypeError, ValueError)` on already-typed floats** → dropped.\n- **Two-location lookup for the same field** → `option_index` is canonically\n  at `data.detail.option_index`; the validation now reads via\n  `node_detail_value()`.\n- **Magic precision constants applied on both producer and consumer sides** →\n  `round(., 6)` hoisted to `MANUAL_POSTERIOR_OVERRIDE_PRECISION` in\n  `core/node_data.py` and shared.\n- **Reaching past underscore boundaries across modules** → `_ancestor_closure`,\n  `_build_parent_maps`, `_topological_order_or_raise` promoted to public\n  `ancestor_closure` / `build_parent_maps` / `topological_order_or_raise`\n  on `bn_inference.py`; added to `__all__`.\n\n### Boundary adapter\n\n`validate_graph_object_for_marginalisation` adapts the in-memory\n`BayesianGraph` `Node` / `Edge` shapes (`id`, `from_id`, `to_id`) to the\nrecord shape (`node_id`, `from_node`, `to_node`) once at the boundary,\nso the inner validation function only sees one canonical shape.\n\n### Dropped\n\n- The unused `\"option\" -> \"target\"` node-type alias — no upstream produces\n  `type=\"option\"`.\n\n### Tests\n\n- [x] `tests/test_bn_inference.py` — 15 existing + 2 new regression tests:\n  - `test_build_manual_posterior_override_clamps_extreme_inputs_after_round`\n    asserts `posterior_p=1.0` and `posterior_p=0.0` land strictly inside\n    `(0, 1)` at the 6-decimal precision.\n  - `test_disable_manual_posterior_override_preserves_value_for_reenable`\n    captures the renamed function's contract (key preserved, `enabled=False`,\n    no-op on empty payload).\n- [x] `tests/test_graph_marginalisation_validation.py` — 4 existing, unchanged.\n- [x] Wider `bn_inference` / marginalisation / research_driver / posterior /\n  node_data test selection: **140 passed, 4 skipped**.\n\n### Verification\n\n- [x] `ruff check` clean on all touched files\n- [x] `ruff format` clean\n- [x] Pre-commit hooks pass (ruff, mypy, secrets)\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -3",
  "description": "Update PR body"
}
```

Done. PR #449 now contains both the anti-pattern cleanup and both bug fixes, with regression tests.

**Bug 1 — clamp-undone-by-round** (`core/node_data.py`):
- Round first, then clamp to `[10^-PRECISION, 1 - 10^-PRECISION]` so the bounds survive the round and stay strictly inside `(0, 1)`.
- Regression test `test_build_manual_posterior_override_clamps_extreme_inputs_after_round` asserts `posterior_p=1.0` → `0.999999` and `posterior_p=0.0` → `0.000001`.

**Bug 2 — misleading `without_*` name**:
- Renamed to `disable_manual_posterior_override` with a docstring stating the preserve-for-reenable contract.
- Only internal caller updated.
- Regression test `test_disable_manual_posterior_override_preserves_value_for_reenable` captures: key stays, `enabled=False`, `posterior_p` preserved, no-op on empty payload.

21 in-scope tests pass (19 + 2 new); 140 pass in the wider research_driver/inference/node_data selection. PR body updated to flag both bugs explicitly so the reviewer can find them.

## Pattern worth flagging

- The `reopen/pr-XXX-...-20260604` naming on the open chain (and many of the merged ones) means these are **re-runs of previously-merged PRs** that got reverted, then re-landed. 409→432, 410→433, 411→450, 412→435, 414→436 are all reopen pairs.
- The reverts presumably happened because something on `dev` broke; now the same code is being walked back in without review.
- Net effect: a lot of code is changing rapidly on `dev` with no second pair of eyes, and the anti-patterns we flagged on #432 are the kind of thing that's almost certainly present in the other UI/FS PRs too.

Want me to pull any of these locally so we can start reviewing? My recommendation: start small with the tail of the stack (435, 436, 444, 443) to clear quick wins, then tackle the bigger ones (437, 441, 446) before they cascade into reverts again.

}

  "description": "Categorize reverts"
}

```
Those are pre-existing (not in the diff). Let me also examine the create_graph_edge endpoint regarding the bug where `from_node` is checked for question membership but `to_node` is not.

Looking at create_graph_edge in the diff: `_question_id_for_node(from_node.node_id, ...) != question_id` is checked but `to_node`'s question_id membership is NOT checked. That's a real bug — you can create an edge whose to_node belongs to a different question.

Also, `_question_graph_records` in validate/marginalise filters edges by `from_node in q_node_ids AND to_node in q_node_ids`, but uses string strip on `from_node`/`to_node` which are already typed (`str`). The defensive wrapping.

Let me also check whether `dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)` mutable default issue in MarginalisationValidationIssueRead. That's fine with Field.

Let me check the `_sync_posteriors_from_graph` function — it uses `getattr(mem_node, "posterior_p", None)` and `getattr(mem_node, "data", {})` on `BayesianGraph.nodes` values which have known types (Node class). The `graph: Any` anti-pattern.

Also `evaluate_target_posteriors(graph_obj, ..., write_back=True)` already writes posteriors back to `graph_obj.nodes`. But then `_sync_posteriors_from_graph` uses original on-disk `nodes` (not the `q_nodes`/edges-filtered set). It updates them in place from graph_obj.nodes. This works, but baseline_p is missing from the sync.

Also `node_id = generate_id() if callable(generate_id) else f"{question_id}_{node_type}_{len(nodes) + 1}"` — this fallback uses `len(nodes)+1` as a counter which can collide if a node was previously deleted. Also `getattr(repo, "generate_node_id", None)` is the stringly-typed method lookup anti-pattern.

Same for `generate_edge_id`.

Let me check `delete_graph_edge`: `question_id = str((edge.data or {}).get("question_id") or "").strip()` — fine. But defensive `or ""` chains.

Let me confirm where `set_manual_posterior_override` doesn't validate the question exists.

Now I have enough to write the final report.

## PR #433 Review

### Anti-patterns

- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1109-1110, 1140`** — Defensive string wrapping on comparisons. `str(request.node_type or "").strip().lower()` and `str(request.edge_type or "CONDITIONAL").strip().upper()` on already-typed Pydantic `str` fields. (Rule: "Defensive string wrapping on comparisons".)
- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1066, 1226, 1233`** — `str(edge.from_node or "").strip()`, `str(edge_data.get("question_id") or "").strip()` over typed `EdgeRecord.from_node: str`. Same rule.
- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1170, 1171, 1174, 1175`** — `str(from_node.type or "").strip().lower()` over typed `NodeRecord.type: str`. Same rule.
- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1153, 1224`** — `getattr(repo, "generate_node_id", None)` / `getattr(repo, "generate_edge_id", None)` followed by `if callable(...)`. (Rule: "Stringly-typed method lookup via `getattr(obj, "method_name", None)`".)
- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1098-1107, 1117-1118`** — `graph_obj: BayesianGraph` is typed, but `_sync_posteriors_from_graph` reads `getattr(mem_node, "posterior_p", None)` and `getattr(mem_node, "data", {}) or existing.data or {}`. (Rule: "`graph: Any` when `BayesianGraph` exists" / "Defensive `getattr` + `.get(...) or default` chains on typed dataclass / TypedDict returns".)
- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1102, 1105, 1163, 1200, 1252, 1396, 1545`** — `round(..., 6)` literal duplicated across producer/consumer sites. (Rule: "Magic precision constants applied on both producer and consumer sides".) Same `0.001`/`0.999` clamp literals repeated at `1147` and `1389`.
- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1146, 1199, 1252, 1294, 1388, 1450`** — Naive `datetime.now(UTC).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")` repeated six times. Should be a shared helper. (Rule: "Datetime JSON serialisation as `+00:00`" intent — the helper exists but is being open-coded.)
- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py` create/delete/patch handlers** — Repeat the same five-call load (`load_graphs/load_nodes/load_edges/load_questions`) in every endpoint, with no bundle abstraction. Boilerplate copy-paste smell, mirrors the "per-file `_storage_config` helpers" rule.
- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1450, 1455, 1551-1554`** — `dict(posterior_result.get("diagnostics") or {})`, `list(posterior_result.get("target_posteriors") or [])`, `str(posterior_result.get("status") or "ok")` — defensive `.get() or default` chains on a TypedDict-like return from `evaluate_target_posteriors`. (Rule: "Defensive `getattr` + `.get(...) or default` chains on typed returns".)
- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1530`** — `raise HTTPException(...)` for inference errors; per codequality.md, services should raise typed `DomainError` (e.g. `ValidationError`) and let middleware translate. (Rule: "Raising bare `ValueError`/`Exception` from services or handlers" — generalised to "use the typed hierarchy".) Same for every `HTTPException` raised in the new endpoints.
- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1530`** — `except ValueError as exc:` swallows a programmer error (the `bn_inference` module raises `ValueError` for both "no targets" and "unsupported mode" semantics) and converts it to a user-facing `invalid` response. (Rule: "Blanket `except` … hides programmer bugs".) Compounded by the missing `from exc` chain on the response build.
- **`packages/contracts/graphs.py:236-237, 244-246`** — `target_posteriors: list[dict[str, Any]]` and `diagnostics: dict[str, Any]` in API DTOs. Throws away the typed shape that `evaluate_target_posteriors` already produces. (Rule: "Typed-Pydantic → `dict.get` → typed-TypedDict bridges".)
- **`tests/test_graph_editing_api.py:1-6`** — Imports `_build_client` (private underscore helper) from `tests.test_api_service` across module boundaries. (Rule: "Importing private `_underscore` helpers across module boundaries" — flagged in the sibling PR.)
- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1100, 1102`** — `posterior is None and existing.posterior_p is None: continue` then `round(float(posterior or 0.0), 6)` — the `or 0.0` is dead by construction (guarded above) and silently swaps `None` for `0.0` if the guard regresses. (Rule: "Defensive `.get(key, magic_default)` against a dict whose keys are guaranteed by an upstream invariant".)
- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1119, 1124`** — `replace(graph, node_ids=node_ids)` mutates a list passed by reference into the frozen dataclass; `node_ids = list(graph.node_ids or [])` — `GraphIndex.node_ids: list[str]` is non-optional, the `or []` is folklore. Same at 1132.

### Bugs

- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1166-1170` (create_graph_edge) — Missing `to_node` question check.** Only the `from_node`'s question_id is validated (`_question_id_for_node(from_node.node_id, …) != question_id`), but `to_node` is not. **Trigger:** `POST /api/questions/Q1/graph/edges` with `from_node_id` belonging to Q1 and `to_node_id` belonging to Q2. **Consequence:** A cross-question conditional edge is created; downstream `_question_graph_records` filters it out (so Q1 silently loses the edge), Q2's graph gains a phantom edge from a non-member node, and the per-question marginalisation walks a graph the user never edited.
- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1153, 1224` — Fallback ID generator collides after deletes.** `f"{question_id}_{node_type}_{len(nodes) + 1}"` / `f"{question_id}_edge_{len(edges) + 1}"`. **Trigger:** create node → delete node → create node. **Consequence:** The new node reuses the deleted node's ID (same `len(nodes)+1`), and any stale reference (e.g. an edge that wasn't fully cleaned up, a UI cache, a posterior history record) now points at the wrong row. Compounded by the global counter (`user_q_1_driver_5` for Q1 is not isolated from Q2 if global node count is used as a counter elsewhere).
- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1440-1455` (run_question_marginalisation) — Posterior write-back persists overrides from `_sync_posteriors_from_graph` but discards `baseline_p` and other node fields that `evaluate_target_posteriors(write_back=True)` may have touched.** `_sync_posteriors_from_graph` only copies `posterior_p` and `data`, but the in-memory `BayesianGraph` may carry mutations to other fields. Additionally, `data=dict(getattr(mem_node, "data", {}) or existing.data or {})` at `:1105` **silently falls back to `existing.data` when `mem_node.data` is empty** — meaning if `bn_inference` legitimately cleared a key in `data`, the persisted record still has it. **Trigger:** marginalise a graph after an override is cleared in-memory but the on-disk record still carries it. **Consequence:** Override state on disk diverges from inference state.
- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1098-1107` — `_sync_posteriors_from_graph` skips nodes outside the question.** `lookup` is built over *all* `nodes`, but only nodes present in `graph_obj.nodes` (i.e. this question's nodes) get updated. That's correct, *but* the function name promises a full sync and the function is the only place that persists posteriors after `write_back=True`. **Trigger:** none directly, but it's brittle — if a future caller passes a `BayesianGraph` spanning multiple questions, the lookup-by-id collides silently across questions sharing a node_id prefix.
- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1146-1149` — `baseline = round(max(0.001, min(0.999, float(baseline))), 6)`** silently clamps user input without telling them. **Trigger:** `POST /graph/nodes` with `baseline_p=0`. **Consequence:** Stored value is `0.001`, response says `created: True`, user thinks `0` was accepted. Should `422` instead.
- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1267-1276` (patch_graph_edge_weight) — `weight_history` records `edge.weight` (could be `None` since `EdgeRecord.weight: float | None = None`) as the `old` value.** **Trigger:** patch an edge whose `weight` was never set. **Consequence:** History entry has `old: None`, downstream consumers that assume a numeric old value blow up.
- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1551-1554` — Final `GraphMarginalisationRunResponse` omits the `issues` field on success path.** The DTO defaults `issues: list[...] = Field(default_factory=list)`, but only the error branches populate it. Not a bug today, but combined with the response_model's `extra="forbid"` and the silent-success path, any soft warnings from `evaluate_target_posteriors` (e.g. `clamped_edge_count > 0`) are discarded.
- **`services/api/routes/graphs.py:1003-1008` (patch_graph_node addendum)** — `if question_id and (snapshot["changes"] or snapshot.get("edge_changes"))` then mark stale — but this runs **before** persistence; if the subsequent `save_graph_store_bundle` raises, the question is marked stale even though the graph wasn't saved. **Trigger:** disk write failure during patch. **Consequence:** Stale flag set, edit lost — user sees "you have unsaved changes" on a graph that never moved.
- **`tests/support/api_runtime.py:120` — Test fixture changed `from_node="user_q_1_drv_ghost"` to `from_node="user_q_1_drv_1"`, removing the orphan-edge coverage that `test_delete_graph_node_removes_synthetic_recovered_nodes` previously relied on.** The replacement `_seed_orphan_ghost_edge` adds it back as `edge_5`, but `test_delete_graph_node_removes_incident_edges_and_protects_core_nodes` now asserts `removed_edge_count == 2` because deleting `drv_1` removes the new `edge_4` too — meaning **the test for "ghost edge survives unrelated deletes" was silently weakened**.

## PR #450 review — UI: Analyst graph editor and marginalisation UI

Note: working tree currently reflects post-main-sync state where PR #450 has been folded out, so review is against the diff and the file at merge commit `ed0495ea`.

### 1. Anti-patterns / hygiene issues

**Python tests**

- `tests/frontend/test_frontend.py:2247-2313` — four new tests each re-do `(PROJECT_ROOT / "frontend" / "analyst" / "index.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")` (15 occurrences file-wide now). Codequality.md **"Per-file `_storage_config(tmp_path)` helpers instead of a pytest fixture"** rule: hoist `analyst_html` / `components_css` / `graph_css` to `tests/conftest.py` as pytest fixtures.
- `tests/frontend/test_frontend.py:2252-2254, 2311-2313` — `assert "graph-table-field" in css or "graph-table-field" in (PROJECT_ROOT / ...).read_text(...)`; file I/O inside the `assert` short-circuit obscures intent and only runs lazily.
- `tests/frontend/test_frontend.py:2270, 2271, 2276, 2294, 2308` — heavy `html.split("function X(", 1)[1].split("function Y(", 1)[0]` string-archaeology to slice JS function bodies. Repeated in five places per file with overlapping ranges. Tightly couples tests to source whitespace/ordering; mirrors the brittle-string-assertion shape called out in sibling PRs.

**Frontend hygiene**

- `index.html:2070, 2088, 2090, 2092` — primary node/edge creation UI is `window.prompt("Label for new driver?")` / `prompt("Source node id?")` / `prompt("Target node id?")` / `prompt("Edge weight?", "1.5")`. Browser-native modal dialogs as production UI.
- `index.html:1019, 1051, 1055-1056` — analyst state lives on `window.ELIJAH_API_KEY` and `window.analystState`. Globals-on-`window` pattern.
- `index.html:1057-1063, 1071-1082` — `setGraphEditLocked` does five `document.getElementById` calls per invocation, plus three more `document.querySelectorAll` sweeps in `syncGraphTableEditLock`. Repeated DOM queries with no caching; the IDs are static and could be captured at init.
- `index.html:2083, 2084, 2145` — `.catch(console.warn)` swallows API failures to the dev console with no user surface.
- `index.html:1318, 1321, 1323; 1739-1750; 1757-1765` — markup built via template-literal `innerHTML` (`nodeTableBody.innerHTML = sortedDisplayNodes.map(...)`). User content is wrapped in `escapeHtml`/`escapeAttr`, but one missed call is XSS — fragile. Prefer DOM construction.
- `graph.css:336-338, 360-362, 367, 501-504; components.css:216-218, 222` — `#ef4444`, `#fecaca`, `rgba(239, 68, 68, …)` hardcoded in 6+ rules across two files. No `--c-danger-rgb` variable even though `var(--c-driver-rgb)` etc. already exist. Magic colours + duplicated rules across components.css and graph.css.
- `graph.css:347-355, 380` — `.graph-edit-toolbar { z-index: 4 }` while `.sidebar-edge-toggle { z-index: 130 }`. Magic z-index values, no documented scale.
- `index.html:573` — `<div id="cy" role="img" aria-label="Question causal graph" aria-hidden="true">`. `role="img"` + `aria-hidden="true"` is self-contradicting (previous version used a descriptive aria-label without aria-hidden). Accessibility regression.
- `index.html:1339` — `const nextValue = field.tagName === "TEXTAREA" ? field.value : field.value;` — both ternary branches identical. Dead code.
- `index.html:1095` — `panel.textContent = issues.map(...).join("\n")` paired with `white-space: pre-wrap` (graph.css:364). Encoding list semantics into text via newlines instead of building DOM nodes.
- `ui_tooltips.js:2218-2222` — tooltip text keyed by `aria-pressed="true"` attribute string read at runtime. Couples copy to ARIA state.

### 2. Bugs

- **`index.html:1042-1043` — `hasManualPosteriorOverride` treats absence of `enabled` as truthy.** Predicate is `override.enabled !== false && override.posterior_p != null`. Trigger: user toggles Fix off and the server PATCH `/manual-posterior-override` response omits `enabled` from the payload (returning only `posterior_p`). Consequence: `applyManualPosteriorOverrideLocally` writes the payload, but the predicate keeps reporting "fixed". Row stays in `.manual-posterior-override` class, red border + `readOnly` state persists, and `syncGraphTableEditLock` won't re-enable the input until the next full graph refresh.

- **`index.html:1232-1248` — `saveNodeTableField` silently no-ops when the posterior row is not `manual-posterior-override`.** It returns without throwing, but `handleGraphTableFieldCommit` line 1349 has *already* set `field.dataset.initialValue = nextValue`. Trigger: user types a posterior value into a row whose class is removed between focus and blur (e.g. concurrent refresh, race with the Fix toggle). Consequence: typed value is dropped, `initialValue` records the dropped value as committed, the failure catch never fires, no toast — silent data loss.

- **`index.html:1349-1354` — `handleGraphTableFieldCommit` updates `initialValue` synchronously before `await`-ing the save.** Trigger: user tabs through several fields while the network is slow; a second commit on the same field lands before the first promise resolves. Consequence: the rollback path (line 1351-1352) restores the *post-second-edit* `initialValue`, not the truly-saved value.

- **`index.html:2105-2123` — `runMarginalisationBtn` ignores `issues` returned alongside `valid: true`.** The handler checks `!validateResult.ok || validatePayload.valid === false` and otherwise charges through to marginalise. Trigger: validate endpoint returns `{valid: true, issues: [<warning>]}`. Consequence: warnings are not shown; analyst gets no signal that marginalisation ran with concerns.

- **`index.html:1119-1123, 2052, 2145` — `refreshGraphForActiveQuestion` reads `analystState.refreshGraphForQuestion`, assigned only after `wireQuestionAndRunDropdowns` awaits past line 2052.** Trigger: any non-posterior table edit (label/description/baseline) made before that IIFE awaits past line 2052 — possible because the table is rendered during init by `renderGraphTables()` at line 4101's `setDisplayMode("graph")` chain. Consequence: `refreshGraphForActiveQuestion` short-circuits at line 1121, the API write succeeded but the UI shows stale data with no error.

- **`index.html:2092` — `Number(window.prompt("Edge weight?", "1.5"))` returns `0` on cancel.** Currently caught by `weight <= 0`, but the guard couples cancel-handling to a numeric range check. If anyone later relaxes the range guard, Cancel becomes "submit weight 0".

- **`index.html:2088` — `const sourceId = activeGraphNodeId || window.prompt("Source node id?");`.** If the user has an active node selected, the prompt is skipped and `activeGraphNodeId` is used as source unconditionally; but the prompt for `targetId` accepts any free-text string. Trigger: user types a non-existent node id into the target prompt. Consequence: API call goes through with a bad id; failure path surfaces only the server's error message via `showMarginalisationIssues` — no client-side validation against `shim.nodes`.

## PR #433 — Graph editing & marginalisation API

### Anti-patterns (cited)

- **Defensive string wrapping** (`str(x or "").strip().lower()/.upper()`) on Pydantic-typed `str` fields and typed `NodeRecord`/`EdgeRecord` fields — `services/api/routes/graphs.py:1066, 1109-1110, 1140, 1170-1175, 1226, 1233`.
- **Stringly-typed method lookup** — `getattr(repo, "generate_node_id", None); if callable(...)` at `:1153, 1224`.
- **`graph: Any` / defensive `getattr` chains** on the typed `BayesianGraph` in `_sync_posteriors_from_graph` (`:1098-1107, 1117-1118`).
- **Magic precision constants** — `round(., 6)` duplicated at `:1102, 1105, 1163, 1200, 1252, 1396, 1545`; clamp literals `0.001`/`0.999` at `:1147, 1389`.
- **`datetime.now(UTC).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")`** open-coded six times instead of a shared helper (`:1146, 1199, 1252, 1294, 1388, 1450`).
- **Typed-Pydantic → `dict[str, Any]` bridges** — `target_posteriors: list[dict[str, Any]]` and `diagnostics: dict[str, Any]` in `packages/contracts/graphs.py:236-237, 244-246` throw away the shape `evaluate_target_posteriors` already produces.
- **Defensive `.get(...) or default`** on TypedDict-like returns (`:1450, 1455, 1551-1554`).
- **`raise HTTPException`** in service-layer handlers — codequality says use typed `DomainError` subclasses; the new endpoints raise raw `HTTPException` ~15 times. Also `except ValueError → invalid response` (`:1530`) swallows programmer bugs that `bn_inference` raises for "unsupported mode".
- **Cross-module private-helper import in tests** — `tests/test_graph_editing_api.py:1-6` imports `_build_client` from `tests.test_api_service`.

### Bugs

- **Missing `to_node` question-membership check** (`:1166-1170`, `create_graph_edge`) — only the `from_node`'s `question_id` is checked. Trigger: POST `/api/questions/Q1/graph/edges` with `to_node_id` belonging to Q2. Consequence: a cross-question edge is silently created; Q1's filtered marginalisation drops it, Q2 gains a phantom edge from a non-member. **Real bug.**
- **Fallback ID generator collides after deletes** (`:1153, 1224`) — `f"{question_id}_{node_type}_{len(nodes) + 1}"`. Trigger: create → delete → create. Consequence: new node reuses the deleted node's id; any stale referrer (history record, UI cache) now points at the wrong row. **Real bug.**
- **Silent clamp on `baseline_p`** (`:1146-1149`) — `baseline = round(max(0.001, min(0.999, float(baseline))), 6)`. Trigger: POST with `baseline_p=0`. Consequence: stored `0.001`, response says created, user thinks `0` was accepted. Should `422`.
- **`weight_history` records `None` as old weight** (`:1267-1276`) — `EdgeRecord.weight` is `float | None`. Trigger: patch an edge whose weight was never set. Consequence: history entry has `old: None`, downstream consumers that assume numeric blow up.
- **Stale-flag set before save** (`patch_graph_node`, `:1003-1008`) — stale mark runs before `save_graph_store_bundle`. Trigger: disk write fails. Consequence: question marked stale even though graph didn't move; edit lost, UI claims unsaved changes on unchanged graph.
- **`data=dict(...) or existing.data or {}`** in `_sync_posteriors_from_graph` (`:1105`) silently falls back to `existing.data` when `mem_node.data` is empty. Trigger: inference legitimately clears a key. Consequence: cleared key reappears on disk; in-memory and persisted state diverge.
- **Test coverage silently weakened** — `tests/support/api_runtime.py:120` changed the ghost-edge `from_node` so the "orphan edge survives unrelated deletes" assertion no longer exercises an orphan. The replacement fixture changes the expected `removed_edge_count` for the related test.

---

## PR #450 — Analyst graph editor UI

### Hygiene

- **Repeated `read_text()` in tests** (`tests/frontend/test_frontend.py:2247-2313`) — 15 occurrences of the same `index.html` read across the file. Per codequality.md's "per-file helpers vs pytest fixtures" rule: hoist to a fixture.
- **String-archaeology slicing of JS function bodies** in five places per test (`:2270, 2271, 2276, 2294, 2308`) — `html.split("function X(", 1)[1].split("function Y(", 1)[0]`. Brittle, breaks on any whitespace edit.
- **Primary UI via `window.prompt`** (`index.html:2070, 2088-2092`) — node-label / source-id / target-id / edge-weight all use native `prompt()`. Production UI.
- **Globals on `window`** (`:1019, 1051, 1055-1056`) — `window.ELIJAH_API_KEY`, `window.analystState`.
- **Repeated DOM queries with no caching** (`:1057-1063, 1071-1082`) — `setGraphEditLocked` does five `getElementById` calls per invocation on static IDs.
- **`innerHTML` with template literals** (`:1318-1323, 1739-1750, 1757-1765`) — wraps content in `escapeHtml`/`escapeAttr` but one missed call is XSS.
- **Hardcoded colour `#ef4444` / `#fecaca`** repeated 6+ times in `graph.css:336-504` and `components.css:216-222` despite existing `var(--c-driver-rgb)` pattern.
- **Magic z-index** values (`graph.css:347-380`) — `4` vs `130` with no documented scale.
- **Accessibility regression** (`index.html:573`) — `role="img" aria-label="..." aria-hidden="true"` on the same element is self-contradicting (aria-hidden wins, the label is dead).
- **Dead ternary** (`:1339`) — `field.tagName === "TEXTAREA" ? field.value : field.value` (both branches identical).
- **`.catch(console.warn)`** swallows API failures with no user surface (`:2083, 2084, 2145`).

### Bugs

- **`hasManualPosteriorOverride` treats absent `enabled` as truthy** (`index.html:1042-1043`) — predicate `override.enabled !== false && override.posterior_p != null`. Trigger: PATCH response omits `enabled`. Consequence: row stays in `.manual-posterior-override`, readonly persists until full refresh. **Real bug.**
- **`saveNodeTableField` silently no-ops** when row class changed mid-edit (`:1232-1248`), while `handleGraphTableFieldCommit:1349` has already updated `initialValue`. Trigger: concurrent refresh or race with Fix toggle. Consequence: silent data loss, no toast. **Real bug.**
- **`initialValue` updated before `await`** (`:1349-1354`) — rollback path restores the post-second-edit value if a second commit lands before the first promise resolves.
- **`runMarginalisationBtn` ignores `issues` when `valid: true`** (`:2105-2123`) — analyst gets no signal that marginalisation ran with warnings.
- **`refreshGraphForActiveQuestion` reads `analystState.refreshGraphForQuestion` before it's assigned** (`:1119-1123, 2052, 2145`) — table renders during init; early edits short-circuit at `:1121`, API write succeeds but UI shows stale data with no error.
- **`Number(prompt("Edge weight?", "1.5"))` returns 0 on Cancel** (`:2092`) — currently masked by `weight <= 0` guard; cancel-handling and range-validation are tangled.

---

## Recommendation

Open **two follow-up cleanup PRs** off `dev`, both draft:

1. **#433 cleanup + bugs** — type the API routes against `NodeRecord`/`EdgeRecord` Protocols, lift the `round(., 6)` and timestamp helpers, fix the `to_node` check, the ID collision, the silent baseline clamp, and the stale-flag-before-save. Includes regression tests for each bug.
2. **#450 cleanup + bugs** — pytest fixture for the html/css read, fix the override `enabled` predicate, silent no-op data-loss, race-on-`initialValue`, ignored-issues-on-valid. CSS hygiene can be a smaller pass.

The #433 cleanup is the higher-leverage one (server bugs, real data integrity). #450 is mostly UI states that recover on refresh — still worth fixing but lower risk than the API bugs.

Want me to start on #433 cleanup first?

```
@router.post("/questions/{question_id}/graph/validate-marginalisation", response_model=GraphMarginalisationValidateResponse)
def validate_question_marginalisation(
    question_id: str,
    storage_adapters: WorkerStorageAdapters = Depends(get_storage_adapters),
    graph_store_paths: GraphStorePaths = Depends(get_graph_store_paths),
) -> GraphMarginalisationValidateResponse:
    nodes = storage_adapters.graph_store_repository.load_nodes(graph_store_paths.nodes_path)
    edges = storage_adapters.graph_store_repository.load_edges(graph_store_paths.edges_path)
    q_nodes, q_edges = _question_graph_records(question_id=question_id, nodes=nodes, edges=edges)
    issues = validate_graph_for_marginalisation(question_id=question_id, nodes=q_nodes, edges=q_edges)
    reads = [_validation_issue_to_read(issue) for issue in issues]
    return GraphMarginalisationValidateResponse(
        question_id=question_id,
        valid=not reads,
        issues=reads,
    )


@router.post("/questions/{question_id}/graph/marginalise", response_model=GraphMarginalisationRunResponse)
def run_question_marginalisation(
    question_id: str,
    config: ApiConfig = Depends(get_api_config),
    storage_adapters: WorkerStorageAdapters = Depends(get_storage_adapters),
    graph_store_paths: GraphStorePaths = Depends(get_graph_store_paths),
) -> GraphMarginalisationRunResponse:
    graphs = storage_adapters.graph_store_repository.load_graphs(graph_store_paths.graphs_path)
    nodes = storage_adapters.graph_store_repository.load_nodes(graph_store_paths.nodes_path)
    edges = storage_adapters.graph_store_repository.load_edges(graph_store_paths.edges_path)
    questions = storage_adapters.graph_store_repository.load_questions(graph_store_paths.questions_path)
    q_nodes, q_edges = _question_graph_records(question_id=question_id, nodes=nodes, edges=edges)
    issues = validate_graph_for_marginalisation(question_id=question_id, nodes=q_nodes, edges=q_edges)
    if issues:
        reads = [_validation_issue_to_read(issue) for issue in issues]
        return GraphMarginalisationRunResponse(
            question_id=question_id,
            status="invalid",
            issues=reads,
        )

    graph_obj = _build_question_bayesian_graph(
        question_id=question_id,
        config=config,
        storage_adapters=storage_adapters,
        graph_store_paths=graph_store_paths,
        graphs=graphs,
        nodes=nodes,
        edges=edges,
    )
    try:
        posterior_result = evaluate_target_posteriors(graph_obj, question_id=question_id, write_back=True)
    except ValueError as exc:
        reads = [
            MarginalisationValidationIssueRead(
                code="inference_error",
                message=str(exc),
                subject_id=question_id,
                subject_type="question",
            )
        ]
        return GraphMarginalisationRunResponse(question_id=question_id, status="invalid", issues=reads)
```

| 451 | 09:14Z | +52/-8 (4f) | UI: Local dev compose and question catalog guards | Duplicate of #435 (different branch, identical title/size). |

## #452 — paddy chased our rename, but introduced a real issue

The good part: they updated `services/api/routes/graphs.py` to call `disable_manual_posterior_override` (matching our #449 rename). Without this, the API would have broken on import. So functionally it had to land.

The bad part: same PR added this in `_question_graph_records`:
```python
q_edges = [
    replace(edge, type=str(edge.type or "").strip().upper() or None)
    for edge in edges
    if str(edge.from_node or "").strip() in q_node_ids and str(edge.to_node or "").strip() in q_node_ids
]
```

Two things going on:
1. **It's a band-aid for a real bug we introduced.** Our #449 cleanup replaced the defensive `str(getattr(edge, "type", None) or "").strip().upper()` in `graph_marginalisation_validation.py` with a direct `edge.type != "CONDITIONAL"` compare, on the assumption that `edge.type` is canonically uppercase. Paddy hit a code path where it isn't (legacy fixture, JSON load, something) and patched at the API filter — so the validation receives normalised edges and our cleanup still works in that flow. But any other flow that calls `validate_graph_for_marginalisation` directly with non-canonical edges will silently skip them.
2. **It re-introduces the exact anti-pattern we just cleaned up.** Per codequality.md's "DB-loaded vs file-loaded same field with different conventions" rule, the right fix is to canonicalise once at the persistence write boundary (in `packages/storage/graph_store.py` when `EdgeRecord` is constructed), not at every read site.

So #452 needs to be followed up by:
- Find where `EdgeRecord.type` gets set non-canonically.
- Canonicalise at the boundary (uppercase + strip on construction).
- Drop #452's band-aid from `_question_graph_records`.
- Keep our direct `edge.type == "CONDITIONAL"` compares in validation.

## Open PRs still on deck

| # | Title | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 437 | FS3: Add global domain-control foundation | OPEN |
| 438 | FS3: Replace domain packs with domain control | OPEN |
| 439 | FS3: Add whole-document recall extraction | OPEN |

| 413, 415, 416 | UI mega-PRs | OPEN |
| 436 | UI: Postgres co-tenant | CLOSED (presumably folded into 437+) |

These are still in the stack and likely to merge over the next few hours unless you intervene.

## What I'd do right now

Pick one of these — I'm ready to act:

1. **Open the cleanup PR for the merged ones** (consolidates #433 + #450 + #452 fixes — anti-patterns + bugs we found, with regression tests). Branch off `dev`, draft. ~1–2 hours of work.
2. **Race the stack**: quickly look at #444 + #445 + #443 (small chain links, total ~480 lines) and review-block them before they self-merge.
3. **Both in parallel** — I review the small open ones (#443/#444/#445) while a subagent drafts the cleanup PR.
4. **Ping paddy first** — message them to pause merges so we can catch up. Otherwise we're chasing a moving target.

My instinct: #4 is the real fix and you should do that out-of-band. While that's in flight, I'd start option #1 because the merged bugs (especially #433's missing `to_node` question check and the ID collision) are data-integrity issues that don't get less bad by waiting.

# PR #461 Review Report

## 1. Cleanup preservation check

### `core/node_data.py` — REGRESSED across the board
- ⚠️ **`MANUAL_POSTERIOR_OVERRIDE_PRECISION` constant: missing.** `grep MANUAL_POSTERIOR_OVERRIDE_PRECISION` returns nothing anywhere in the PR tree. The constant we added in PR #449 was deleted.
- ⚠️ **`build_manual_posterior_override`: clamp/round order regressed** (`/tmp/pr461.diff` lines ~315-321). The body is `clamped = min(1.0 - 1e-9, max(1e-9, float(posterior_p))); return {..., "posterior_p": round(clamped, 6), ...}` — clamps first, then rounds. The original #432 bug is back: an input of `1 - 1e-9` becomes `0.999999999` after clamp, then rounds to `1.0` (or `0.0` at the low end), losing the clamp.
- ⚠️ **`disable_manual_posterior_override` rename reverted.** The exported helper is `without_manual_posterior_override` again (`/tmp/pr461.diff` ~340). The behaviour also drifted: in PR #449 the helper preserved `posterior_p` so the override could be re-enabled with the same value; the new body just flips `enabled=False` only when an override already exists, otherwise it's a no-op (does not preserve any prior value either). It's a behavioural regression *and* a name regression.

### `core/research_driver/graph_marginalisation_validation.py` — file replaced with regressed version
The PR adds this file as `new file mode` (`/tmp/pr461.diff:291-293`), overwriting the clean version on `origin/dev` (which has Protocols, public-name imports, no defensive wrapping). Replaces it with:
- ⚠️ **Lost `MarginalisationNode` / `MarginalisationEdge` Protocols.** Every helper now takes `node: Any` / `edge: Any` (`/tmp/pr461.diff:316, 343, 359, etc.`). Direct violation of codequality.md `graph: Any when BayesianGraph exists`.
- ⚠️ **`str(getattr(edge, "type", None) or "").strip().upper()`** at `_edge_type` (`/tmp/pr461.diff:358-362`). Codequality "Defensive string wrapping on comparisons". Same anti-pattern on every `_node_id`, `_node_label`, `_node_type`, `_edge_from`, `_edge_to`, `_edge_id`.
- ⚠️ **Multi-fallback chains** like `getattr(edge, "edge_id", None) or getattr(edge, "id", "")`, `from_node/from_id`, `to_node/to_id` (`/tmp/pr461.diff:354, 368, 372`). Codequality "Multi-way fallback chains without a named authoritative field".
- ⚠️ **`try/except (TypeError, ValueError)` on typed `float | None`** in `_node_baseline_p` and `_edge_weight` (`/tmp/pr461.diff:346-349, 380-383`). Reintroduces the exact pattern #449 removed.
- ⚠️ **Direct dict bypass for `option_index`**: `_node_data(node).get("detail", {}).get("option_index")` (`/tmp/pr461.diff:551-553`) instead of `node_detail_value()`. PR #449 specifically routed this through the canonical lookup.
- ⚠️ **Boundary adapter wrapper gone.** `validate_graph_object_for_marginalisation` is now `nodes = list((getattr(graph, "nodes", {}) or {}).values())` (`/tmp/pr461.diff:573-577`). The clean version adapted in-memory `Node`/`Edge` to `MarginalisationNode`/`MarginalisationEdge` records; this version just stuffs them into the `Any` pipeline.

### `core/research_driver/bn_inference.py` — REGRESSED
- ⚠️ **Names underscored again.** Current file has `_ancestor_closure`, `_build_parent_maps`, `_topological_order_or_raise` (`core/research_driver/bn_inference.py:151,166,190`). PR #449 made these public. They are now private again and `__all__ = ["evaluate_target_posteriors"]` (`bn_inference.py:656`) — only one symbol exported.
- ⚠️ **`MANUAL_POSTERIOR_OVERRIDE_PRECISION` not imported / not used.** `_write_posteriors_to_graph` writes `round(float(...), 6)` magic-literal on lines 540, 542 — the consumer side of the cross-side precision contract is back to a hardcoded literal. Codequality "Magic precision constants applied on both producer and consumer sides".
- ⚠️ **Docstring vs code drift.** The new docstring claims "Inference write-back skips nodes with active manual overrides or IW clamps" (`/tmp/pr461.diff:135-141`), but `_write_posteriors_to_graph` (`bn_inference.py:524-542`) only checks `iw_trigger.verdict`, not `manual_posterior_override`. The override path the docstring documents is not implemented — silent broken contract.
